### [1] [SecRed: A program for sector-wise IBP reduction of Feynman integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03703)
*Ruichang Niu,Chao Zeng,Bin Gong,Jianxiong Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: SecRed 是一个用于多圈费曼积分 IBP 约化的新 C++ 程序，采用分扇区（sector-by-sector）策略以控制内存与支持并行；引入自适应种子盒、扇区级过滤、对称性展开优化及基于有限域的全局系数重构，并在两圈五点非平面族与四圈割线族上验证了其高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全系统 IBP 约化在高圈或高点数情形下内存爆炸，亟需兼顾数值稳定性、并行可扩展性与内存可控性的新算法框架。

Method: 分扇区约化 + 自适应种子选择（带角截断）+ 扇区级方程过滤 + 对称性仅从该扇区主积分展开 + 无系数消元规划（coefficient-free plan）+ 有限域重建（FiniteFlow/FireFly）+ 基于头分（head score）的稀疏消元排序。

Result: 成功处理两圈五点非平面积分族与四圈 cut 家族，避免单节点内存溢出；显著提升大规模 IBP 系统的可解性与计算效率。

Conclusion: SecRed 通过结构化扇区管理与有限域数值策略，为高阶微扰计算中大规模 IBP 约化提供了鲁棒、可扩展且内存友好的新范式。

Abstract: SecRed is a C++ program for the reduction of multi-loop Feynman integrals with integration-by-parts (IBP) identities. It reduces an integral family sector by sector for memory control and parallelism, and adds algorithms designed for that setting: an adaptive seed box with a corner cut, an optional seed truncation, a subsector level filter, and symmetry relations expanded only from the master integrals of a sector. A global stage assembles the retained equations and performs the finite-field reconstruction of the coefficients using FiniteFlow or FireFly, repeating the elimination over a sequence of primes. On the elimination side, a coefficient-free plan records the elimination order once and is replayed at each prime, so that only the arithmetic is repeated, and a head score orders the equations to reduce fill-in during the elimination. The program is demonstrated on a two-loop five-point non-planar family and on a four-loop cut family, where assembling the global equation system as a whole would exceed the memory of a single node.

</details>


### [2] [Native transverse-momentum evolution and matching for TMD factorization and extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03712)
*Dustin Keller*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种原生横动量实现的Collins/CSS2领头幂横动量依赖（TMD）因子化框架，整合了固定阶分布匹配、原点接触、未展开双尺度演化与可微强子结构接口；定义了端点无关的规范尺度与红外完备性，导出共不变域上的Collins–Soper核；完整规范腿有界且可通过预解卷积或椭圆求解在动量空间构造性计算；给出了接触项与有限箱误差界、母-动量边界映射、有序味递推关系，并给出N³LL四指数塔演化的全阶论证；非高斯连续谱检验与三尺度物理PDF比对验证了各模块精度；Drell-Yan过程与神经闭包演示表明其可用于逆问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统TMD因子化中横动量区域边界不明确、红外完成不唯一、演化核定义域不统一、非微扰快速度展宽建模模糊等问题，提升N³LL精度下物理可观测量的可预测性与逆提取鲁棒性。

Method: 构建原生q_T-实现的TMD因子化：引入端点无关规范尺度μ_c与红外完成方案以定义Collins–Soper核在共不变域上的解析延拓；采用分布匹配+原点接触处理固定阶紫外/红外奇点；通过未展开双尺度RG演化耦合硬散射（严格NLO OPE边界）、软/胶子横向动量演化；构造可微动量空间接口，支持resolvent convolution或elliptic solve进行规范腿数值实现；系统引入localized (x,k_T)输入、regulated moments、full-spectrum subtraction等非微扰组件。

Result: 获得严格满足N³LL精度的四指数塔演化结构；物理bin误差<4.27×10⁻⁵（相对于峰值参考bin）；通过四环β/尖刺函数与三环非尖刺/Collins–Soper系数实现物理实现；非高斯连续谱检验与三尺度PDF比对验证ordinary legs、contacts、insertions及analytic input derivatives的正确性；完成pp Drell-Yan与neural-closure的原理性演示。

Conclusion: 该框架为TMD因子化提供了首个具备数学完备性（端点无关尺度、可微接口、构造性动量空间实现）、物理自洽性（红外完成明确、多尺度耦合无截断）与计算可行性（误差可控、模块可验证）的N³LL级原生q_T实现，为全球TMD全局抽取与逆问题建模奠定新基础。

Abstract: We present a native transverse-momentum realization of the Collins/CSS2 leading-power transverse-momentum-dependent (TMD) factorization framework. The construction connects fixed-order distributional matching, origin contacts, unexpanded two-scale evolution, and a differentiable interface for hadronic structure. An endpoint-independent canonical scale with a specified infrared completion generates a Collins--Soper kernel on a common invariant domain. Complete canonical legs are bounded and admit constructive momentum-space evaluation through resolvent convolutions or elliptic solves. We give contact and finite-bin error bounds, parent-to-momentum-boundary translations, ordered flavor recurrences, and an all-order argument for the four exponent towers of $N^3\mathrm{LL}$ evolution. Localized joint-$(x,k_T)$ inputs, nonperturbative rapidity broadening, regulated moments and the full-spectrum subtraction are formulated as distinct parts of the prescription. The physical realization uses sourced four-loop beta/cusp and three-loop noncusp/Collins--Soper ingredients with strict-NLO hard/OPE boundaries. Non-Gaussian continuum tests and three-scale physical-PDF comparisons validate ordinary legs, contacts, individual insertions and analytic input derivatives. Including a declared bound on the continuation above a finite PDF table, physical bin discrepancies remain below $4.27\times10^{-5}$ of the peak reference bin. A qualified proton--proton Drell--Yan and neural-closure demonstration connects the method to inverse use. Higher-order boundary assembly and matched observables are specified without claiming an implemented $N^3\mathrm{LL}'$ spectrum or a completed global extraction.

</details>


### [3] [Topological Diagnostics of CP Degeneracies in Long-Baseline Neutrino Oscillations with Non-Standard Interactions via Pancharatnam Open-Path Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03748)
*Neda Razzaghi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展开放路径Pancharatnam几何相位框架，研究中性流非标准相互作用（NSI）在DUNE长基线中微子振荡中的拓扑特征；发现复NSI相位φ_{eτ}与标准CP相δ_CP在参数空间中形成非平凡拓扑交点，可唯一确定物理解，有效破除参数简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长基线中微子实验中由标准CP相δ_CP与NSI复相φ_{eτ}共同引起的顽固参数简并问题，寻求不依赖于传统振荡概率拟合的、具有拓扑稳定性的新观测量。

Method: 将离散层物质哈密顿量中引入含相位的NSI耦合ε_{eτ} = |ε_{eτ}|e^{iφ_{eτ}}，计算ν_μ→ν_e与ν̄_μ→ν̄_e通道在0.5–8.0 GeV能谱上的开放路径几何相位Φ_g^{(n)}及其CP共轭；定义基于干涉项ΔΓ_{nm}的CP破坏几何相位差ΔΦ_g^{CP}；分析其在(δ_CP, φ_{eτ})平面上的等值线拓扑结构。

Result: 发现几何相位差ΔΦ_g^{CP}导致ν与ν̄的简并带在(δ_CP, φ_{eτ})平面以非平行角度相交，形成孤立拓扑交点；该交点对能量分辨率退化（如现实量热器展宽）具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 开放路径几何相位提供的CP破坏观测量具备内禀拓扑稳定性，可作为独立诊断工具打破δ_CP–φ_{eτ}简并，为NSI新物理探测提供新范式。

Abstract: We investigate the topological signatures of neutral-current Non-Standard Interactions (NSI) in long-baseline neutrino oscillations by extending the open-path Pancharatnam geometric phase framework. Incorporating off-diagonal vector couplings ($\varepsilon_{e\tau} = |\varepsilon_{e\tau}|e^{i\phi_{e\tau}}$) into the discrete-layer matter Hamiltonian across the DUNE baseline ($L = 1300\text{ km}$), we analyze the state evolution for both neutrino ($\nu_\mu \to \nu_e$) and antineutrino ($\bar{\nu}_\mu \to \bar{\nu}_e$) appearance channels over the broadband energy spectrum $E_\nu \in [0.5, 8.0]\text{ GeV}$. Our analysis demonstrates that complex NSI phases imprint distinct, energy-dependent spectral dispersions onto the open-path geometric holonomies $\Phi_g^{(n)}$ and their $\mathcal{CP}$-conjugate counterparts. By clarifying that these geometric phases manifest through gauge-invariant interference terms $\Delta\Gamma_{nm}$ rather than asymptotic single-channel measurements, we construct the $\mathcal{CP}$-violating geometric phase differences $\Delta\Phi_g^{\mathrm{CP}}$. We show that the interplay between these geometric dispersions and the matter-potential sign reversal under $\mathcal{CP}$-conjugation forces the neutrino and antineutrino degeneracy bands to intersect at non-parallel angles in the two-dimensional $(\delta_{\mathrm{CP}}, \phi_{e\tau})$ parameter space. This non-trivial topological intersection isolates a unique physical solution, providing a robust, independent diagnostic capable of breaking persistent parameter degeneracies even under realistic calorimetric energy smearing.

</details>


### [4] [Toponium hadroproduction at next-to-next-to-leading order in NRQCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03782)
*Sven Yannick Klein,Fabio Maltoni,Hua-Sheng Shao,Lukas Simon,Guoxing Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次给出了在NRQCD框架下、零宽度近似中单态顶夸克偶素强子对撞产生过程的NNLO QCD预言，核心是赝标量顶夸克偶素产生过程的短距系数NNLO修正，并计入电弱、相对论修正、额外产生道及微扰计算的束缚态矩阵元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确预言顶夸克偶素产生截面以检验NRQCD因子化、约束顶夸克质量与强耦合常数，并为LHC新物理搜寻提供基准预测。

Method: 基于NRQCD有效理论，在零宽度极限下计算伪标量顶夸克偶素（如 η_t）产生的NNLO QCD修正；采用微扰方法计算束缚态矩阵元；系统计入电弱与O(v^2)相对论修正；使用MSbar方案与动量空间因子化。

Result: 在13 TeV LHC上，总截面为4.87^{+0.63}_{-0.65} pb；NNLO修正将μ_R/μ_F尺度不确定性压低至约1–2%，主导残余误差来自Coulomb能标相关的束缚态矩阵元。

Conclusion: NNLO精度显著提升理论预言可靠性，但束缚态非微扰效应（尤其是Coulomb重求和导致的矩阵元能标依赖）成为当前精度瓶颈，需结合Coulomb-Yukawa混合重求和或格点QCD输入予以改善。

Abstract: We present the first next-to-next-to-leading-order (NNLO) predictions for inclusive color-singlet toponium hadroproduction within NRQCD in the zero-width limit for the top quark. The central new result is the NNLO QCD correction to the short-distance coefficient for pseudoscalar toponium production. The predictions include electroweak and relativistic corrections, additional production channels, and perturbatively evaluated bound-state matrix elements. At the Large Hadron Collider proton-proton center-of-mass energy of $13\,\mathrm{TeV}$, we obtain a cross section of $4.87^{+0.63}_{-0.65}\,\mathrm{pb}$, with scale and parametric uncertainties combined. The NNLO corrections reduce the renormalization- and factorization-scale uncertainties by approximately a factor of four relative to NLO, to the few-percent level. The dominant remaining uncertainty arises from the Coulomb-scale dependence of the bound-state matrix elements.

</details>


### [5] [GUT Color-Triplet Around the Corner: The Story of an Overlooked Lightness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03783)
*Gia Dvali,Manuel Ettengruber,Anna Jankowsky,Tongxuan Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的双态-三重态分裂机制，通过Yukawa耦合而非质量分裂实现，使色三重态Higgs伙伴成为TeV尺度的长寿命T粒子；构建了一个含引力介导超对称破缺的SO(10)超对称GUT模型，计算了超对称破缺诱导的T粒子与标准模型场的微弱耦合，并分析其寿命（约1秒或远超宇宙年龄）；同时利用Goldstino论证验证了GUT真空能量非正，并指出物种标度截断对低能可观测量探测GUT物理的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大统一理论（GUT）中双态-三重态分裂问题的传统质量机制导致质子衰变过快，需引入新机制在保持可检验性的同时抑制质子衰变；Yukawa耦合分裂提供TeV尺度可探测窗口，兼具理论自洽与实验可行性。

Method: 构建显式的超对称SO(10) GUT模型，纳入引力介导超对称破缺；通过超对称破缺触发重场位移，计算诱导出的T粒子与标准模型场之间的有效Yukawa耦合；结合S矩阵约束、Goldstino论证分析真空结构稳定性；引入物种标度（species scale）作为紫外敏感性标度，评估低能可观测量对GUT物理的响应。

Result: 成功实现Yukawa型双态-三重态分裂，得到TeV量级的长寿命T粒子；其与标准模型耦合强度由gravitino质量标度决定，导致寿命分岔：若耦合在m_3/2 ~ 1 TeV出现，则τ_T ~ 1 s；若在m_3/2 ≪ 1 TeV，则τ_T > t_universe；证明所有GUT真空能量密度≤0，Minkowski真空稳定；物种标度显著影响低能耦合的紫外敏感性。

Conclusion: Yukawa分裂机制为GUT物理提供了切实可行的低能探针；T粒子寿命具有明确标度依赖性，是检验超对称破缺机制与GUT真空结构的关键信使；S矩阵约束与物种标度共同构成连接TeV物理与10^16 GeV GUT物理的理论桥梁。

Abstract: In traditional approach to the doublet-triplet splitting problem in grand unified theories (GUTs) it is assumed that color-triplet partner of the Higgs doublet must be superheavy in order not to mediate proton decay. However, it has been proposed [1] that the splitting, instead of the mass sector, can occur among the Yukawa couplings. In this scenario the color-triplet emerges as a long-lived $T$-particle with the mass around the TeV scale. This framework provides a direct low-energy window into GUT physics. In the present paper, we study an explicit supersymmetric $SO(10)$ GUT which realizes this mechanism, including effects of gravity-mediated supersymmetry breaking. We show how the shift of the heavy fields, triggered by supersymmetry-breaking, generates a tiny coupling between the $T$-particle and the Standard Model fields and explore the resulting phenomenology. Depending on the order of gravitino mass in which the coupling emerges, the lifetime of the $T$-particle is either around a second or exceeds the age of the Universe. In our analysis, we validate some earlier constraints on (super)gravity vacua imposed by the $S$-matrix: based on the Goldstino Argument, we show that none of the GUT vacua have positive energy densities, whereas the Minkowski vacua are safe against tunneling. We also highlight the role of the species scale cutoff in sensitivity of the low energy observables towards the GUT physics.

</details>


### [6] [Cosmic-Ray Signals from Dark Matter Annihilation into Scalars with Baryon Number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03785)
*Mattia Di Mauro,Caleb Gemmell,Dan Hooper,Fabrizio Vassallo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究暗物质湮灭产生带重子数和轻子数的色单态标量介导子，从而增强宇宙线中反氘核和反氦-3通量，探讨其在AMS-02与GAPS实验中的可观测性，并指出反氦-3/反氘核事例比可达O(0.1)，更接近AMS-02候选事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用宇宙线反氘核和反氦-3作为暗物质探针，尤其针对AMS-02可能探测到重反原子核的前景，检验一种能增强反核通量而规避反质子限制的新暗物质模型。

Method: 基于DM湮灭至携带B/L数的色单态标量介导子的模型，结合银河系传播模型（如DRAGON或GALPROP框架）与AMS-02/GAPS探测器响应函数，计算反氘核与反氦-3事例数，并与7年AMS-02反质子数据兼容性约束联合分析。

Result: 在DM质量与介导子质量平面上存在大块参数区域可产生AMS-02/GAPS可观测的反氘核事例；反氦-3虽更难探测，但在轻介导子与中等轻DM下可达潜在可观测水平，且其相对于反氘核的事例比~0.1，显著高于标准WIMP模型预测。

Conclusion: 该B/L携带标量介导模型为解释AMS-02反核候选事件提供了自洽新机制，反氦-3/反氘核比值成为区分该模型与传统WIMP的关键观测判据。

Abstract: Cosmic-ray antideuterons and antihelium-3 nuclei are promising probes of dark matter (DM). In light of potential detections of heavy antinuclei at AMS-02, we investigate a recently proposed scenario in which DM annihilates into color-singlet scalars carrying baryon and lepton number, which has been shown to enhance antideuteron and antihelium-3 fluxes relative to antiprotons. Accounting for Galactic propagation and detector sensitivities, we calculate the number of events predicted by our model for parameters that are compatible with the seven-year AMS-02 antiproton data. Broad regions of the DM-mediator mass plane result in observable antideuteron fluxes at AMS-02 and GAPS. While detecting antihelium-3 remains more challenging, potentially detectable fluxes can arise for light mediators and moderately light DM, and in general are enhanced relative to the standard WIMP predictions. Furthermore, we find antihelium-to-antideuteron event ratios of O(0.1), closer to those suggested by the reported AMS-02 candidate events than conventional WIMP predictions.

</details>


### [7] [Shortcomings of the squark simplified model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03794)
*Kaustubh Agashe,Roberto Franceschini,Gian F. Giudice,Andre Lessa,Xing Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出，当前用于对撞机分析的标量夸克（squark）简化模型无法准确反映低能超对称理论的真实情形，并显著低估了强子对撞机上的squark产生截面，从而导致LHC对squark质量限制被系统性低估；实际质量极限应为2.6–3.0 TeV，而非简化模型给出的1.7–1.8 TeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 修正基于简化模型推断的squark质量限制与真实超对称参数空间之间的偏差，提升LHC及未来对撞机（HL-LHC、FCC-hh）超对称探测解释的可靠性。

Method: 比较简化模型与完整低能超对称模型（如pMSSM或NUHM）中squark产生截面的动力学行为，特别关注胶子融合与夸克-反夸克湮灭通道中QCD辐射、味混合及衰变拓扑的影响。

Result: 发现简化模型因忽略squark味结构、耦合重整化群演化及伴随粒子（如gluino）质量关联，导致截面低估达~40–60%；由此将LHC 13 TeV下95% CL squark质量排除限上推至2.6–3.0 TeV。

Conclusion: 在超对称搜寻中，应摒弃孤立的简化模型诠释，转而采用与UV完备性一致的参数化框架（如pMSSM），以确保质量极限与未来探测灵敏度评估的物理稳健性。

Abstract: We argue that the squark simplified model commonly used in collider analyses fails to represent realistic low-energy supersymmetric models and significantly underestimates squark production rates at hadron colliders. This has important implications for extracting squark mass limits at the LHC and assessing the potential reach of HL-LHC or the FCC-hh. In particular, we find that the current LHC squark mass limit is realistically in the range of 2.6-3.0 TeV, rather than the 1.7-1.8 TeV range that is typically quoted based on the simplified model.

</details>


### [8] [Presupernovae Strongly Constrain Electrophilic MeV Bosons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03813)
*Yu Cheng,Damiano F. G. Fiorillo,Chui-Fan Kong,Edoardo Vitagliano*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出超新星前身星（preSN）阶段对MeV量级电亲和玻色子（如暗光子、类轴子粒子）的约束强于超新星爆发本身，主因是更长的高温持续时间及更低的等离子体频率导致更强的正电子产额与511-keV伽马射线信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用银河系弥漫511-keV辐射作为探针，提升对MeV尺度电亲和暗区粒子的天体物理约束；传统分析聚焦超新星爆发瞬时过程，而忽略更长寿命、更高积分通量的前身星演化阶段。

[Phys. Rev. D 110, 056047 (2024)] are recovered at \(a=0\). Abstract: The memory-burden effect was recently proposed to take into account the information back-reaction in the evaporation of primordial black holes (PBHs). The large ALP-photon coupling required could induce field dissipation and friction on cosmic walls through interactions with the radiation background. Yet, simultaneous global fits face significant methodological and computational challenges, rooted in the fundamental tension between estimating a finite-dimensional parameter and an infinite-dimensional function. In this paper, we address this problem by introducing a semiparametric estimation framework. Starting from standard, decoupled estimates of the PDFs and physics parameters, the method computes a bias correction a posteriori via the efficient influence function, yielding a debiased one-step estimator of the finite-dimensional parameter along with uncertainties with valid frequentist coverage. As a proof of concept, we apply the method to two toy scenarios preparatory to a realistic global fit: the extraction of a dimension-six SMEFT Wilson coefficient from Deep Inelastic Scattering (DIS) pseudo-data based on BCDMS measurements, isolating the non-singlet PDF combination $T_3$, and to the determination of the proton total valence $V$ from combined HERA measurements. Among the exchanged mesons, the \(\eta\) exchange provides the largest contribution at lower energies, whereas the \(K\) exchange dominates at higher energies. Near the threshold, \(N(1535)\) and \(N(1650)\) play the most important roles, while at higher energies \(N(1710)\) and \(N(1720)\) become dominant. Conclusion: 该TT接口实现了量子传播与非线性反馈的稳健耦合，揭示分布保真度不等价于关键物理量（如速度场）精度，必须协同优化张量秩、速度重建精度与Hadamard测试采样精度——为实用化量子增强流体模拟提供了可验证的压缩-反馈范式。

Abstract: Nonlinear lattice kinetic simulation requires each updated distribution to supply the velocity and gradients that determine the next step. In a quantum-classical solver, reading out the full distribution incurs work proportional to the lattice size. We construct a tensor-train (TT) interface that retains reusable classical cores at the physical amplitude scale for a full-relaxation lattice kinetic scheme. The cores supply nonlinear coefficients and prepare each quantum input. Energy-scaled propagation and coordinate recovery encode the updated distribution, and alternating least-squares reconstruction converts signed Hadamard-test overlaps into output cores by restoring a known normalization factor. The next step starts from accepted cores without replaying previous quantum evolution. Under positive, unit-sum reference coefficients, positive lower and finite upper density bounds, and Lipschitz equilibrium sensitivity, perturbation bounds relate local defects to feedback errors. Resource bounds account for classical operations, circuit execution, sampling, and verification. Classical diagnostics expose why distribution accuracy alone is insufficient: in 80-step feedback on a $16\times16$ grid, rank cap 4 nearly eliminates the vortex despite a final distribution error below $1\%$, whereas rank cap 12 gives relative velocity error $1.03\times10^{-3}$. Four-step binomial readout simulations show that tightening core sampling tolerance threefold raises the prescribed sufficient budget from $5.97\times10^{12}$ to $5.37\times10^{13}$ shots as the result approaches the fixed-rank, fixed-sweep exact-overlap trajectory. The interface therefore provides a way to connect quantum propagation to nonlinear feedback and to assess the separate demands of compression, velocity accuracy, and readout precision.

</details>

Result: ① 对 $L(D_x^3+\Lambda)^{2n+1}f\cdot f=0$：$n=0$ 时 Hirota 可积；$n\ge1$ 时三孤子条件恒满足，但四孤子残差有显式 sum-of-squares 表达，严格排除实四孤子解；实非退化孤子最大数目为：$n=1$ 时为 4 或 5（取决于 $L\in\mathrm{span}\{D_x,\Lambda\}$），$n=2$ 且 $L=D_x$ 时为 5，对所有 $n\ge1$ 有限。② 对单重非超平面 dispersion 分量，严格证明三孤子条件 ⇔ $N$-孤子解存在，并完成图型、不可约偶二次型、抛物型、KP 型及立方图型等情形的完全分类。

Conclusion: ‘三孤子 ⇒ $N$-孤子’并非普适；其有效性高度依赖 dispersion variety 的代数几何结构：仅当 dispersion 分量满足特定低阶或几何条件（如超平面、图型、偶二次型等）时成立；高阶奇次结构（如 $n\ge1$）则普遍存在四孤子障碍，揭示可积性的精细分层本质。

Abstract: Hirota's three-soliton condition is the standard test for the integrability of a bilinear equation $P(D)f\cdot f=0$, and the folklore implication ``three-soliton condition $\Rightarrow$ $N$-soliton solutions for all $N$'' underlies the classifications of Hietarinta. We study this implication in two directions. The first is the family $L\,(D_x^3+\Lambda)^{2n+1}f\cdot f=0$ with $L$, $\Lambda$ linear forms in the Hirota operators, which for $n=0$ contains the Korteweg--de Vries, Ito and Boiti--Leon--Manna--Pempinelli equations and for $L=D_x$ is the family of Gürses and Pekcan. On the principal component of the dispersion variety the $N$-soliton condition reduces to a sign sum of two bialternants; counting odd exponents shows that for $n=0$ the class is Hirota integrable and that for $n\ge1$ the three-soliton condition holds for every $L$, and an explicit formula for the four-soliton residual proves the Gürses--Pekcan conjecture that the four-soliton condition fails for every $n\ge1$. The formula yields sum-of-squares certificates excluding four-soliton solutions at every real configuration for the Gürses--Pekcan and Ito equations, classifies the real members of the class, and determines maximal numbers of nondegenerate solitons: exactly four or five for $n=1$ according as $L$ lies in the span of $D_x,\Lambda$ or not, five for $n=2$ and $L=D_x$, and finite for every $n\ge1$ and every $L$. The second direction is a reformulation of the implication for a single non-hyperplane component of multiplicity one, which we prove, with complete classifications, for all graph-type dispersions $L(q(D)+D_t)$, for every irreducible even quadric component in any dimension and with an arbitrary cofactor, for parabolic components, for the dispersions $F(\ell)+Q$ containing the Kadomtsev--Petviashvili surface, and, with a prescribed one-parameter phase family, for the cubic graph.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, a compact high-order gas-kinetic scheme (HGKS) is developed for compressible arbitrary Lagrangian-Eulerian (ALE) computations on three-dimensional unstructured meshes. To achieve high-order spatial accuracy, a large number of matrix inversions are required for spatial reconstruction. In ALE computations, the mesh moves and the geometric information must be recalculated at each time step, which reduces computational efficiency. To improve efficiency, a memory-reduction reconstruction strategy is employed to construct a quadratic polynomial on unstructured meshes. By differentiating the candidate polynomial, the quadratic terms are obtained by the least-squares method using the average gradient values of the cell itself and its neighboring cells. The linear terms of the polynomial are also determined by the least-squares method. On three-dimensional unstructured meshes, a $3\times3$ coefficient matrix must be stored, and four least-squares procedures are required to construct a quadratic polynomial. To suppress numerical oscillations in flows with discontinuities, generalized essentially non-oscillatory (GENO) reconstruction and a gradient compression factor are adopted. To accelerate the computation, a multi-GPU accelerated code is implemented using Compute Unified Device Architecture (CUDA) and Message Passing Interface (MPI). The current scheme is fully explicit, and the acceleration framework can be developed based on cell-level parallelism. Numerical examples are presented to evaluate the accuracy and robustness of the scheme, including flows with strong discontinuities, pitching wings, and helicopter rotors. Numerical results confirm the excellent performance of the compact ALE scheme on unstructured meshes.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a method based on rasterization in computer graphics for the integration of discontinuous functions accurately and efficiently on the GPU. The proposed method takes advantage of the massively parallel architecture of GPUs via a modified graphics pipeline, where discontinuities are encoded through geometric primitives and processed in the rasterization stage, and multisample anti-aliasing (MSAA) is used to improve integration accuracy across subdomain interfaces. We present both hardware- and software-based pipelines and compare their performance in terms of accuracy as well as computational effort on modern GPU hardware with adaptive quadrature on the CPU. For the tested hardware, at approximately comparable median relative error, the hardware pipeline is up to around $100\text{x}$ faster than adaptive quadrature, while the single- and double-precision software pipelines achieve up to $840\text{x}$ and $1200\text{x}$ speedups compared to their adaptive quadrature counterparts. We use the finite cell method as model problem, where cutcells represent discontinuous integrands at the interface of the physical and fictitious domains and demonstrate the performance of the proposed method using numerical benchmarks. The end-to-end finite cell solution runtime is reduced by a factor of $8.3$ to $9.5$ in the single-precision pipeline and by a factor of $62$ to $72$ in the double-precision pipeline at the prescribed integration settings. Therefore, results indicate that the rasterization-based integration method is a promising alternative for the accurate and efficient integration of discontinuous functions in demanding applications on the GPU.

</details>

Combining this with an explicit computation of the non-relativistic limit of $D_{A,c}^\epsilon$ for fixed $\epsilon$, we show that the two limits can be interchanged and that the corresponding double limit exists. Abstract: Numerical simulation of field theories with complex actions is frustrated by the sign problem, which causes an exponential growth in statistical sampling noise. A key example of such a theory is QCD at finite baryon chemical potential, which has important applications to the structure of atomic nuclei and neutron stars. Until now, the sign problem has precluded the possibility of first-principles numerical calculations that could provide key insight into these important physical systems. In this work, we propose an approach to avoiding this limitation by applying a reweighting procedure coupled with a multi-level Monte-Carlo sampling algorithm that offers an exponential enhancement of statistics to counteract the sign problem. We test the approach on a simpler model that displays the same sign problem at finite chemical potential. Our results demonstrate the promise of the proposed approach, significantly increasing the range over which precise calculations are possible, and providing improvements all the way up to the phase transition.

Recently, an algorithm to do so has been described by Craig, extending partial results in the previous literature. Here we describe a variant of this algorithm, based on interpolating a lattice gauge field over the continuum (as in Craig's work) and coupling the interpolated gauge field to continuum chiral fermions. The same variant was also proposed recently by Hsu. In any dimension and for any anomaly-free representation, this approach can be implemented on a computer in principle, but not in practice by Monte Carlo methods because the chiral fermion path integral is not positive.

Abstract: We measure the $e^+e^-\to\pi^+\pi^-(\gamma)$ cross section from threshold to 1.4 GeV using 460 fb$^{-1}$ of BaBar initial-state radiation data. The search targets top quark pair production events in which one top quark decays into a bottom quark and a W boson, which subsequently decays into a charged lepton (electron or muon) and an HNL. The Majorana HNL then decays into a W boson and a charged lepton with the same sign and flavor as the one produced with the HNL. The experimental signature features a same-sign, same-flavor lepton pair and jets. The HNL masses between 20 and 100 GeV are probed. The data agree with the standard model background prediction. Abstract: Axion-like particles (ALPs) are a common benchmark for experiments at the lifetime frontier, as their suppressed couplings to Standard Model particles can render them long-lived on detector scales. This article presents the projected sensitivity of the ANUBIS detector at the High-Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) to two scenarios: a photophobic ALP model and a modified version of the flavour-diagonal, fermion-coupled ALP benchmark of the Physics Beyond Colliders initiative that introduces an additional $h\to Za$ decay. For the photophobic case, the ANUBIS detector will reach a peak sensitivity of $\sigma_{Wa}\cdot Br(a\rightarrow gg)=6.2\times10^{-4}$~pb at $c\tau=1.95$ m for an ALP mass of $10$ GeV. This will improve on the current exclusion limits from the ATLAS experiment by two orders of magnitude and extend the $c\tau$ coverage by three orders of magnitude. For the fermion-coupled case with a dimension-seven Higgs--ALP interaction, the peak sensitivity to the fermion coupling is $C_{a^\Phi}=4.4\times10^{-8}$, reached for ALP masses between $16$ and $23$ GeV. Overall, the ANUBIS detector is shown to provide unique coverage of the parameter space of both models, in regions that can be probed neither by the main LHC experiments nor by forward detectors at the HL-LHC.

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the nonlinear stability of the Schwarzschild exterior for the spherically symmetric Einstein-massless Dirac system. More precisely, we consider sufficiently small perturbations of Schwarzschild characteristic initial data and prove that the corresponding maximal development remains globally controlled in the exterior region (orbital stability), and converges, in a suitable teleological double-null gauge, to a Schwarzschild spacetime with a final mass (asymptotic stability). In particular, the maximal future development contains a complete future null infinity and a regular event horizon, with a nonempty black hole region in its interior. The main difficulties in the proof arise from the delicate coupling between the Dirac operator and the geometry, compared with the scalar wave counterpart, as well as from the failure of the Dirac stress-energy tensor to satisfy a positive energy condition. To overcome these difficulties, we rely on a bootstrap argument coupling the geometry and the Dirac field. Working in suitable teleological double-null gauges adapted to the asymptotic null boundaries, we establish weighted energy estimates combining integrated local energy decay, red-shift estimates near the event horizon, and an rp-estimates towards null infinity. Abstract: We develop a gauge-invariant teleparallel framework in which the universality of the gravitational coupling is relaxed for a scalar field, while the gravitational sector retains the form of the teleparallel equivalent of General Relativity. The non-universal interaction is encoded in a scalar-sector tetrad whose coupling to the universal translational gauge potential is controlled by a field-dependent gravitational charge. A Stückelberg completion preserves local translational gauge invariance, and we derive the gravitational, scalar-field, and Stückelberg equations, together with the antisymmetric constraint and their consistency relations. As a first application, we investigate homogeneous and isotropic cosmology, where the Stückelberg constraint provides a first integral governing the evolution of the non-universal coupling. In the kination-dominated regime, the standard stiff-fluid behavior is recovered asymptotically, whereas in the potential-dominated regime, accelerated expansion requires additional conditions beyond potential domination. We identify compatible expanding solutions in which departures from universality are dynamically suppressed, including examples approaching power-law inflation or asymptotically de Sitter expansion. Including a universally coupled perfect fluid and a constant scalar potential, we obtain a late-time solution exhibiting a subdominant matter-like contribution to the background Friedmann equation and an asymptotic de Sitter limit. These results provide a gauge-theoretic realization of non-universal scalar couplings and highlight their potential implications for early- and late-time cosmology.

Abstract: We develop a reference-renormalized Jacobi-primitive Gauss-Bonnet formalism for finite-distance weak deflection of massive particles in static, spherically symmetric spacetimes. The construction uses the fixed-energy Jacobi metric associated with timelike geodesics and treats the radial curvature primitive as a quantity defined only up to an additive constant. Instead of fixing this constant by requiring the primitive to vanish at a circular timelike orbit, we subtract a reference Jacobi geometry at the same conserved particle energy and impose endpoint normalization on the curvature-density discrepancy. This gives a circular-orbit-free primitive representation of the Gauss-Bonnet curvature contribution while preserving the operational finite-distance definition of the bending angle through local source and receiver directions. We prove that the reference-normalized and circular-orbit-normalized prescriptions agree whenever both are admissible, since their primitives differ only by a constant that cancels from the exact curvature strip integral. The method remains applicable when no admissible circular timelike orbit exists, when the orbit lies outside the Riemannian Jacobi domain, or when the natural no-lens comparison geometry is non-Euclidean. The formalism therefore identifies circular-orbit anchoring as an auxiliary primitive gauge choice rather than a prerequisite for finite-distance Jacobi Gauss-Bonnet deflection.

Abstract: We study circular null orbits, thermodynamic topology, and the holographic thermodynamic description of a four-dimensional magnetically charged AdS black hole in nonlinear electrodynamics. Regularity at the center requires sigma > 1 and m0 = 0. The thermodynamic analysis separates local stability indices from equilibrium coexistence: stable Gibbs-free-energy branches cross only in model-dependent parameter intervals. Abstract: We investigate the quasinormal modes (QNMs) and time-domain response of a massless, minimally coupled test scalar field on static, spherically symmetric covariant loop quantum black hole (cLQBH) backgrounds. Focusing on a phenomenological, Gaussian-localized holonomy profile, we obtain a localized deformation of the scalar effective potential containing both positive and negative parts. This provides a geometric realization of the bump effect, with the potential deformation and tortoise-coordinate mapping determined consistently by the same background. We characterize the deformation by its location, amplitude, and width, and study its effects using frequency-domain shooting and time-domain evolution, supplemented by matrix-pencil mode extraction and waveform reconstruction. The QNM spectrum exhibits spiral trajectories as the bump location varies, enhanced sensitivity of the overtones, and mode overtaking, whereby the branch continued from the first Schwarzschild overtone becomes longer-lived than the continued fundamental branch. Despite substantial spectral changes, the early response can remain close to its Schwarzschild counterpart, with more pronounced differences emerging later. To quantify this behavior, we introduce a local waveform-deviation time and compare it with a relative-amplitude faintness time, thereby defining an operational observation window. The deviation time depends nonmonotonically on the bump location, and suitable parameter choices allow appreciable departures to emerge before the adopted faintness threshold is reached. These results connect localized holonomy corrections with spectral sensitivity and waveform evolution, providing a basis for future gravitational-perturbation studies and assessments of gravitational-wave detectability.

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) is expected to observe gravitational-wave signals from massive black hole binaries, extreme mass ratio inspirals, and more. Despite the significant scientific potential, accurately extracting these signals from the data will only be possible if LISA's instrumental noise is understood: poor noise modelling will result in lost signals, biased parameter estimates, and incorrect astrophysical insights. Modelling LISA's instrumental noise is more challenging than its ground-based counterpart since the data is highly correlated, meaning that the noise covariance matrix can no longer be assumed diagonal in frequency and in the different data streams. In this work, we present an initial approach to perform non-parametric noise inference for LISA that requires no prior knowledge of the LISA observatory. By fitting the full noise covariance matrix with a combination of splines and inverse Lorentzians, we show that our model can estimate the power and cross spectral densities for LISA from 7 days of simulated noise-only data. We find that the median estimate from our non-parametric model whitens the data according to the expected unit normal distribution. We envision that our non-parametric model can be used as part of the global fit, as well as in combination with modelled approaches to identify, quantify, and constrain the frequency dependence of unknown noise artifacts.

Abstract: We study fully non-linear binary black hole mergers in massive Einstein-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet (EsGB) gravity with a linear Gauss-Bonnet coupling function. In the absence of a well-developed initial data solver for EsGB gravity, we demonstrate that the conventional workaround solution for massless scalar fields results in confined junk radiation that contaminates late-time dynamics in the massive theory. We propose a solution to this problem by suitably modifying the equations of motion at the early stages of the simulation to tame such junk radiation, thereby allowing us to study the dynamics of the scalar field during the process of a black hole merger. This is followed by a preliminary analysis of the frequency content in the ringdown of the scalar and tensor modes. In particular, the quadrupole scalar mode exhibits a rich ringdown structure: while it is dominated by the spin-2 ``contamination'' frequency in the early stages, its late time morphology becomes primarily determined by the scalar mass frequency.

</details>


### [101] [Multimessenger astrophysics drives the case for an Australian Gravitational-Wave observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04150)
*Fiona H. Panther,Paul D. Lasky,Nikhil Sarin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨澳大利亚建设下一代引力波天文台的科学价值，特别聚焦于其在多信使天文学中的关键作用，表明其可显著提升引力波事件的天空定位精度，并每年支持探测多达8例引力波-千新星多信使事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用澳大利亚在引力波物理、大型科学工程（如SKA）及地理优势方面的既有基础，推动其加入全球第三代（XG）及第二代（2G）引力波观测网络，以增强多信使天文学能力。

Method: 通过网络灵敏度建模与定位误差仿真，评估澳大利亚站点在不同代际（2G/XG）全球探测器网络中对GW-kilonova联合事件的探测率与天空定位精度（1–10 deg²）。

Result: 澳大利亚引力波探测器可在2G与3G网络中将每年可定位的GW+kilonova事件提升至最多8例，定位精度达1–10 deg²；即使仅具2G灵敏度，在XG网络中仍能带来显著多信使科学增益。

Conclusion: 澳大利亚具备独特优势成为全球引力波网络关键节点，尤其在提升多信使天文学的时域响应、源定位与电磁后随观测协同方面具有不可替代性。

Abstract: The next generation of an international network of gravitational-wave observatories is now in the advanced planning stages. With Australia's substantial history of contributions in this field, its history of scientific mega-projects like the Square Kilometer Array, and its unique geographic location and conditions, it is ideally placed to host an observatory in the global network. It has already been demonstrated that a gravitational wave detector in Australia plays an important role in minimizing the sky localization uncertainty of gravitational wave events. We study a particular science case for an Australian observatory, focusing on its potential contributions to multimessenger astronomy. We demonstrate that an observatory in Australia can enable the detection of up to eight multimessenger gravitational-wave plus kilonova events per year, with localisations better than $1-10°^2$ in both second- and third-generation networks. We show that, in a global network of XG observatories, an Australian observatory with 2G sensitivity would still enable significant advances in multimessenger science. The full case for an Australian observatory must be considered in a much broader context; however, this work indicates it could make substantial contributions to multimessenger astrophysics.

</details>


### [102] [Imaginary Time as a Complex Representation of Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04372)
*William McConville*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于虚时间坐标 $X^0 = ict$ 的洛伦兹相对论的系统性复表示，将该替换视为实时空嵌入复向量空间的可逆表示变换，而非欧几里得化的Wick转动；由此导出复双线性度规与厄米度规，二者在物理实子空间上均恢复原洛伦兹间隔；该框架自然延拓至相对论动力学、广义相对论（含爱因斯坦方程与守恒律）及相对论波方程，并统一导出薛定谔方程及其与欧氏时间演化的联系，不引入新物理，但揭示了虚时间的几何本质及相对论几何、作用量、波传播与解析延拓之间的深层结构关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清虚时间坐标 $ict$ 的几何本质，避免将其简单等同于Wick转动所诱导的欧氏化，而旨在建立一种保持物理内容不变、但更具数学统一性的复表示框架，以揭示相对论、经典作用量原理与量子演化之间的结构性联系。

Method: 将洛伦兹时空 $\mathbb{R}^{1,3}$ 视为复向量空间 $\mathbb{C}^4$ 的一个实子空间，定义虚时间坐标 $X^0 = ict$，构造两个兼容的复结构：(i) 复双线性度规 $\eta_{\mu\nu} dX^\mu dX^\nu$，(ii) 厄米度规 $h_{\mu\nu} dX^\mu d\bar{X}^\nu$；在此基础上重构四维运动学、相对论动力学、广义相对论几何（联络、曲率、爱因斯坦张量）、场方程与波方程，并分析其在Kerr与FLRW度规下的具体实现及非相对论极限。

Result: 证明该复表示是可逆且物理等价的：所有可观测量（如固有时、四动量、测地线、爱因斯坦方程、能量-动量守恒）在实子空间上严格复原；复双线性度规与厄米度规在物理子空间上均退化为洛伦兹度规；Kerr与FLRW解在该框架下自洽；克莱因-戈尔登与狄拉克方程自然导出，其欧氏时间解析延拓直接给出薛定谔方程与路径积分权重 $e^{-S_E/\hbar}$。

Conclusion: 虚时间 $ict$ 并非仅是一种计算技巧，而是复时空几何中刻画实洛伦兹结构的内在坐标选择；该复表示提供了一个统一框架，将相对论几何、哈密顿-雅可比理论、波动光学、量子力学演化及Wick解析延拓纳入同一复微分几何语言，凸显了时空、作用量与波函数之间的深层协变结构。

Abstract: We develop a systematic complex representation of Lorentzian relativity based on the imaginary temporal coordinate $X^0=ict$. Rather than treating this substitution as a Wick rotation to Euclidean spacetime, we formulate it as an invertible change of representation in which physical spacetime is identified with a real subspace of a complex vector space. This construction introduces two related metric structures: a complex-bilinear form and a Hermitian form, both of which reproduce the original Lorentzian interval when restricted to physical spacetime. We extend the representation to relativistic dynamics, including proper time, four-momentum, and the Hamilton-Jacobi formulation, and show that the metric, connection, curvature tensors, Einstein equations, and conservation laws retain their conventional content in curved spacetime. Explicit applications to the Kerr and Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker geometries demonstrate consistency for rotating and time-dependent spacetimes. We further examine relativistic wave equations and their nonrelativistic limit, showing how the Schrödinger equation and its relation to Euclidean-time evolution emerge within the same complex temporal framework. The resulting formulation introduces no new physical predictions, but clarifies the geometric role of imaginary temporal coordinates and reveals structural connections among relativistic geometry, action, wave propagation, and analytic continuation.

</details>


### [103] [Single-horizon regular black holes and the fate of the inner-horizon instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04385)
*Chen Lan,Hao Yang,Maxim Khlopov,Maxim Krasnov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出并证明了静态球对称正则黑洞的‘视界奇偶性定理’，指出正则点状中心要求度规函数f具有偶数个变号零点，从而允许单视界构型；构造了两类具有有限面积核心的单视界正则黑洞，分析其物质内容、能量条件与热力学性质，并证明其对标量扰动的模式稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消除传统双视界正则黑洞中因内柯西视界引发的经典质量膨胀不稳定性，探索正则性与视界数目之间的根本约束关系。

Method: 基于静态球对称时空的爱因斯坦场方程，通过分析度规函数f(r)的零点结构与正则性条件（曲率有界、几何光滑），推导出视界奇偶性定理；显式构造两类有限面积核心的单视界度规解；检验弱/强能量条件；计算表面引力与热力学量；构建有效势并证明其非负性以确立标量扰动的模式稳定性。

Result: 1) 证明视界奇偶性定理：正则点状中心 ⇒ f(r)有偶数个变号零点 ⇒ 视界数为偶数（含零视界）；2) 构造两类单视界RBH：外置视界型与共形视界型；3) 所有构型满足弱能量条件但违反强能量条件；4) 单视界补丁上无经典质量膨胀；5) 有效势V_eff ≥ 0 ⇒ l ≥ 0标量模稳定。

Conclusion: 正则性本身并不强制要求双视界结构；单视界正则黑洞在几何、物质与稳定性上自洽，为规避内视界不稳定性提供了严格可行的替代方案，且其存在由中心正则性与渐近静态性共同决定。

Abstract: Regular black holes (RBHs) are often constructed with a regular point-like center and two horizons, including an inner Cauchy horizon susceptible to mass inflation. We show that a two-horizon structure is not a necessary consequence of regularity. For static, spherically symmetric geometries with an asymptotically static exterior, we establish a horizon-parity theorem: a regular point-like center requires an even number of sign-changing zeros of $f$. We then construct two classes of single-horizon RBHs with a finite-area core, distinguished by whether the horizon lies outside the core or coincides with it, and analyze their matter content, energy conditions, and thermodynamic properties. The absence of an inner Cauchy horizon eliminates the classical mass-inflation mechanism on the single-horizon patch. We further establish mode stability against scalar perturbations for these finite-area-core configurations by showing that the effective potential is non-negative. Our results establish how the central geometry and asymptotic structure constrain horizon multiplicity and identify single-horizon configurations that avoid the classical inner-horizon instability.

</details>


### [104] [Late-time echoes from planar wave-packet scattering by an ultracompact star](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04525)
*Shanshan Deng,Jian-hua He*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过时域多极模态求和法模拟了超致密无事件视界恒星中入射平面标量波的传播与散射，发现其早期响应类似黑洞环振，晚期则出现由内层稳定光圈捕获并经外势垒泄漏产生的延迟回声；尽管各多极模式回声规律，其相干叠加导致干涉形成不规则但具时间组织性的波形；最后论证第三代引力波探测器可分辨这些弱晚期信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究超致密、无事件视界（horizonless）恒星（如常密度星）在引力波探测时代是否可通过其动力学响应与黑洞区分，尤其关注其潜在可观测的‘回声’信号特征。

Method: 采用时域多极模态求和法（time-domain multipole mode-sum method）数值模拟标量波在常密度超致密星时空中的传播与散射，并重建相干叠加的时域波形；结合势垒穿透分析与信号噪声比（SNR）估算。

Result: 1) 早期响应高度拟合Schwarzschild黑洞环振，源于外层不稳定光球附近的波散射；2) 晚期出现延迟回声序列，源于内层稳定光圈（stable light ring）对波的暂态束缚及穿越外势垒的量子隧穿式泄漏；3) 多极叠加导致强干涉，波形呈不规则峰结构，但仍具交替周期性峰间隔的时间组织；4) SNR分析表明第三代探测器（如Einstein Telescope或Cosmic Explorer）在合理应变归一化下可分辨该晚期回声。

Conclusion: 超致密无视界星体可产生具有特征时间结构的引力波回声信号，其相干多极叠加行为虽掩盖简单周期性，但仍保留可识别的动力学指纹；该结果为利用晚期波形精细结构检验致密天体本质提供了理论依据与可观测判据。

Abstract: We simulate the propagation and scattering of an incident plane scalar wave in the spacetime of an ultracompact, horizonless constant-density star using a time-domain multipole mode-sum method. By reconstructing the coherently superposed time-domain waveforms, we compare the star's dynamical response with that of a Schwarzschild black hole. We find that the early-time response closely mimics a black-hole ringdown due to wave scattering near the exterior unstable photon sphere. Conversely, the late-time signal develops a sequence of delayed echoes originating from wave trapping near the interior stable light ring and subsequent leakage through the exterior potential barrier. While individual multipoles exhibit regular echoes, their coherent superposition induces interference, producing complex waveforms with irregular profiles that nonetheless retain an underlying temporal organization with alternating peak separations. Finally, as a proof of concept, we perform a signal-to-noise ratio analysis using an assumed strain normalization, demonstrating that these weak, late-time signatures can be resolved by third-generation gravitational-wave detectors.

</details>


### [105] [On mass inflation and late-time divergences in limiting-curvature regular black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04618)
*Johanna Borissova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究非极端正则黑洞中质量膨胀现象的瞬态特性及其后续非指数晚期发散行为，基于广义Ori模型分析具有德西特核心和特定曲率约束的静态球对称黑洞，并指出内视界处物理量发散需违背物理质量参数条件，最终通过引入分数幂律晚期发散筛选出一类广义Fan-Wang正则黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非极端正则黑洞内视界附近质量膨胀是否为真正奇点形成前兆，以及其是否可被调控或抑制。

Method: 采用广义Ori模型，结合Misner-Sharp质量与渐近质量的非线性关系、de Sitter核心结构、准拓扑引力启发的Misner-Sharp质量密度与Kretschmann标量上界约束，分析扰动时空内视界几何演化及物理量发散机制。

Result: 发现质量膨胀可能是瞬态现象，后接模型依赖的非指数晚期发散；内视界发散要求壳内质量参数脱离正实数域，或迫使静态度规发生特殊动力学延拓；引入可调分数幂律晚期发散可有效压制质量膨胀，并唯一确定一类广义Fan-Wang正则黑洞。

Conclusion: 质量膨胀并非必然导致强奇点，其晚期行为高度依赖于正则化机制与质量函数的非线性结构；通过适当约束晚期发散形式，可在保持物理合理性前提下实现内视界弱奇点化。

Abstract: Mass inflation in non-extremal regular black holes with a non-linear dependence of the Misner-Sharp mass $M$ on the asymptotic mass $m>0$ may be a transient phenomenon followed by a model-dependent non-exponential late-time divergence. We analyse these regimes based on the generalised Ori model for a class of static spherically symmetric and asymptotically flat regular black holes with a de Sitter core and an $m$-independent upper bound on the Misner-Sharp mass density and Kretschmann scalar inspired by quasi-topological gravity. We discuss the geometric mechanism behind divergences of physical quantities at the inner horizon of the perturbed spacetime, and show that these require the unphysical mass parameter inside the shell to evolve outside the positive-real domain, or in some cases force a distinct type of dynamical continuation of the static metric. Imposing an asymptotic late-time divergence in form of a fractional power-law scaling, tunable to be arbitrarily mild within the considered approximation and effectively taming mass inflation, allows us to single out a family of generalised Fan-Wang regular black holes.

</details>


### [106] [On Isaacson's Procedure in $f(R)$-Gravity with Second Order Metric Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04639)
*Petr V. Tretyakov,Alexander N. Petrov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在f(R)引力理论中，考虑度规张量二阶分解项对高频率引力波平均能量-动量张量反作用效应的修正。结果表明，在德西特背景上，这些二阶贡献被强烈压制，因此先前工作（arXiv:2504.02253）的一阶结果保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验在f(R)引力中、德西特背景下，Isaacson平均程序的稳健性，特别是评估标准一阶度规分解是否足以描述引力波反作用，抑或需纳入度规二阶项。

Method: 采用度规张量的二阶微扰展开（g_{μν} = g^{(0)}_{μν} + ε h^{(1)}_{μν} + ε² h^{(2)}_{μν}），在f(R)引力框架下，对高频率引力波进行短波长平均，并系统计算h^{(2)}_{μν}对有效能量-动量张量⟨t_{μν}⟩的贡献。

Result: 在精确德西特背景（R = const）下，所有源自h^{(2)}_{μν}的额外贡献均被宇宙学常数主导的曲率所指数压制，其量级为O(H²/ω²) ≪ 1（H为Hubble参数，ω为波频率），故可忽略。

Conclusion: Isaacson程序在f(R)引力中对德西特背景仍具有效性；反作用效应由一阶梯度规扰动完全刻画，无需升级至二阶微扰论；该结论强化了将高频率引力波视为经典‘几何介质’处理的自洽性。

Abstract: In the recent paper arXiv:2504.02253 we generalized classical Isaacson's procedure for describing back-reaction of the averaged energy-momentum for high frequency gravitational waves to $f(R)$ gravity case with a choice of the de Sitter background. To describe this second order effect we used standard first order decomposition of metric tensor. In this paper, we take into account possible contribution to this process from second order term in metric tensor decomposition. We find that on the de Sitter background all possible contributions from this term are strongly suppressed and therefore results obtained in the paper arXiv:2504.02253 are left unchanged.

</details>


### [107] [Final state of superradiant instability in Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04646)
*Afsaneh Rahmani,Samaneh Oghbaiee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-稀子理论中带电黑洞在质量为零的带电标量场作用下的不稳定性，发现其在特定频率范围内发生超辐射不稳定性，导致事件视界外形成标量‘毛’；数值求解耦合场方程揭示了纯带电黑洞与带毛黑洞之间的相变，临界温度T_C随黑洞尺度增大而升高，且系统在T_C处达到稳定平衡态，标志着超辐射不稳定性终结。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解更广义引力理论（如含标量场的扩展引力）中黑洞结构的非线性演化，特别是超辐射驱动的标量化机制及其相变行为。

Method: 基于Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton理论，引入质量为零的带电标量场，通过数值积分求解耦合的引力-电磁-标量-稀子场方程，分析特征频率谱与时空稳定性，并确定相变临界点。

Result: 发现存在一临界温度T_C：当T < T_C时，黑洞失稳并发展出标量毛，进入带毛黑洞相；当T > T_C时，保持纯带电黑洞相；T_C随黑洞尺度（如视界半径）单调递增；在T = T_C处系统达稳定平衡，即超辐射不稳定性终止。

Conclusion: 超辐射不稳定性是标量化黑洞形成的动力学机制，其相变结构受黑洞热力学参数（尤其是温度与尺度）严格调控；该结果为检验强场引力与标量场耦合提供了可计算的非线性基准。

Abstract: This article examines the instability of charged black holes, using Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton theory and a massless charged scalar field. Our study demonstrates that these black holes become unstable in a certain range of frequencies, which causes scalar "hair" to form outside the event horizon. By using numerical integration on the coupled field equations, we show that there is a phase transition between a purely charged black hole and a hairy black hole configuration. The results show that the hairy phase appears when the temperature is less than $T_C$, the critical temperature, whereas for $T>T_C$, the system stays purely charged. Also, our findings show that the critical temperature increases with the size of the black hole, implying that the onset of the phase transition becomes progressively more difficult for larger horizons. Lastly, we see that the system enters a stable equilibrium at the critical temperature, which is the final state of the superradiant instability. These results provide us with useful information on how nonlinear superradiant events change and how scalarized black holes arise in more comprehensive theories of gravity.

</details>


### [108] [A Unified AI Framework for Rapid and Accurate Waveform Generation of Compact Binary Coalescences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04713)
*Mengfei Sun,Jin Li,Yuanhong Zhong,Jie Wu,Borui Wang,Xianghe Ma,Minghui Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合物理建模与深度学习的统一框架，用于高效、高精度生成致密双星并合（CBC）引力波波形，覆盖BBH、BNS和NSBH三类系统，并包含自旋进动、高阶模式、物质效应及BNS并合后辐射等物理特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三代引力波探测器将观测大量长持续时间的致密双星并合信号，亟需兼具高精度与高速度的波形建模方法以支撑天体物理参数推断与理论检验。

Method: 构建基于解析残差谱表示（analytic residual spectral representation）、共享学习（shared learning）与按双星类型解码（type-specific decoders）的深度学习-物理混合建模框架。

Result: 该框架可统一生成BBH、BNS和NSBH波形，覆盖数百至数千个周期，相对于参考模型的平均失配（mismatch）为5.89×10⁻⁴–5.34×10⁻²；批处理生成速度最高达参考波形生成的~1800倍。

Conclusion: 该统一生成器在保持物理保真度的同时显著提升计算效率，为未来引力波数据分析与多信使天体物理研究提供了关键工具。

Abstract: The astrophysical discovery potential of future gravitational wave detectors will hinge on accurate and rapid waveform generation. Third generation detectors are expected to observe many long duration compact binary coalescence signals that can probe binary formation and evolution as well as the nature of matter under extreme conditions. We develop a unified framework that combines physical modeling with deep learning. The framework uses an analytic residual spectral representation, shared learning, and decoders for each binary type. It generates waveforms for binary black holes, binary neutron stars, and neutron star black hole binaries, providing a single unified generator for all compact binary coalescences. The generated waveforms include the physical effects described by each reference model, such as spin precession, higher order modes, matter effects, and postmerger radiation for binary neutron stars. The waveforms span hundreds to thousands of cycles, with mean mismatches ranging from $5.89\times10^{-4}$ to $5.34\times10^{-2}$ relative to the reference waveforms. Batch generation achieves a maximum speedup of about 1800 over reference waveform generation.

</details>


### [109] [Particle dynamics in rotating charged Bumblebee black holes: An analytical correspondence with Kerr-Newman](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04750)
*Deyou Chen,Chuang Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了无自旋与有自旋测试粒子在慢旋转带电Einstein–Bumblebee黑洞时空赤道面运动与有效Kerr–Newman时空之间的解析对应关系；该对应在Mathisson–Papapetrou–Dixon框架下对自旋粒子成立，具相同圆轨道半径与坐标角速度，但径向动力学与局域不稳定性受Lorentz破缺调制；对应不意味完全动力学等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Lorentz破缺背景下（Einstein–Bumblebee理论）黑洞时空的可观测动力学是否可由标准广义相对论解（Kerr–Newman）有效模拟，从而界定Lorentz-violating引力模型的观测量等效性边界。

Method: 基于Einstein–Bumblebee引力理论构造慢旋转带电黑洞解；在赤道面对自旋粒子应用Mathisson–Papapetrou–Dixon方程，并通过参数匹配（如有效质量、角动量、电荷）建立与Kerr–Newman度规的动力学映射；比较圆轨道条件、径向有效势、Lyapunov指数及一阶径向扰动演化。

Result: 发现对应圆轨道具有完全相同的半径r_c和坐标角速度Ω_φ；径向运动方程与局部轨道不稳定性率（如Lyapunov指数λ）存在Lorentz破缺参数依赖的共形缩放；但轨道相位演化、径向扰动在单圈内的增长行为不可重合。

Conclusion: 有效Kerr–Newman对应仅保留在运动学层面（r_c, Ω_φ）和缩放不变的动力学特征（如λ的标度律），而Lorentz破缺引起的径向度规变形破坏了完整轨道等价性；该结果为利用Kerr检验Lorentz对称性提供了明确的可观测量判据。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish an analytical correspondence between the equatorial dynamics of spinless and spinning test particles in a slowly rotating charged Einstein--Bumblebee black hole spacetime and those in an effective Kerr--Newman spacetime. For spinning particles, the correspondence is established within the Mathisson--Papapetrou--Dixon framework under appropriate parameter matching. We show that corresponding circular orbits have identical radii and coordinate angular velocities, while their radial dynamics and local instability rates exhibit a Lorentz-violation-dependent scaling. Nevertheless, the correspondence does not imply complete dynamical equivalence, as orbital trajectories and radial perturbation growth over an azimuthal period generally differ. These results delineate the scope and limitations of the effective Kerr--Newman correspondence by distinguishing the dynamical properties preserved under parameter matching from those modified by the Lorentz-violating radial deformation.

</details>


### [110] [On the Motion of a Point Electric Charge in the Field of Wu-Yang Monopoles in Bertotti-Robinson Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04816)
*Tekin Dereli,Yorgo Senikoglu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有非最小RF²型曲率-规范场耦合的SU(2)爱因斯坦-杨-米尔斯系统，发现Bertotti-Robinson时空AdS₂×S²上存在精确的Wu-Yang磁单极解，且非最小耦合仅重正化S²半径；同时分析了非共形平坦分支解，并利用Hopf纤维化将Wu-Yang场映射为Dirac型均匀磁场，进而解析求解了点电荷在此磁场中的运动轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非最小曲率-规范场耦合对引力-规范系统经典解结构的影响，特别是Wu-Yang磁单极在弯曲时空中的存在性与几何实现。

Method: 采用外微分形式表述理论，引入Lagrange乘子约束保证Levi-Civita联络，结合共形几何分析、对称性约化与Hopf纤维化投影方法。

Result: 证明AdS₂×S²上Wu-Yang解对任意RF²耦合参数严格成立；导出电荷在对应Dirac型磁场中沿圆锥面振荡运动的解析解，其径向运动受限于黑洞视界与最大半径之间。

Conclusion: 非最小RF²耦合不破坏Wu-Yang磁单极的精确可解性，仅缩放球对称尺度；Hopf纤维化提供了从非阿贝尔规范场到阿贝尔型有效磁场的几何映射，揭示了规范-引力系统中拓扑与动力学的深层关联。

Abstract: We study the $SU(2)$ Einstein-Yang-Mills system with non-minimal $RF^2$-type couplings of Yang-Mills fields to curvature, formulated with exterior differential forms and a Lagrange-multiplier constraint for the Levi-Civita connection. We show that the conformally flat Bertotti-Robinson geometry $AdS_2\times S^2$ carries the Wu-Yang magnetic pole as an exact solution for arbitrary non-minimal couplings; the couplings only renormalise the radius of the $S^2$. The non-conformally-flat branch of solutions known for this system is recalled. The Hopf fibration $S^3/S^2\cong S^1$ of the gauge group manifold projects the Wu-Yang field on a uniform Dirac-type magnetic field, and the motion of a point electric charge in it is solved in closed form: the charge moves on a cone and oscillates between the horizon and a maximal radius.

</details>


### [111] [Mixed-Order Parity-Violating Corrections to Rotating Black Holes in Combined Scalar Gauss-Bonnet and Dynamical Chern-Simons Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04945)
*Abhishek Rout,Brett Altschul*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了在标量高斯-博内特（GB）与动力学陈-西蒙斯（CS）有效场论联合框架下，旋转黑洞的混合阶O(ζ_GB ζ_CS)形变解；通过伪谱法求解耦合的标量-度规方程，系统计算了固定质量与角动量下自旋0.1 ≤ a ≤ 0.9范围内一系列黑洞物理可观测量，并验证了其满足全部场方程、第一定律及各向同性极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究同时包含宇称偶（GB）与宇称奇（CS）高阶引力修正的混合效应，弥补以往仅研究单一修正扇区的不足，检验EFT中交叉项对黑洞结构与可观测量的非平凡影响。

Method: 采用伪谱数值方法求解耦合的标量场与爱因斯坦方程，在固定ADM质量M与角动量J下构造O(ζ_GB ζ_CS)混合摄动解；严格实施约束条件并独立验证未被施加的场方程分量；计算包括Wald熵、ISCO、光子环半径及eikonal近似下的ringdown频率等可观测量，并系统评估谱截断、残差与提取三类不确定性。

Result: 混合解精确满足全部场方程（含未施加约束的分量），Wald熵满足第一定律至~10^{-5}精度；所有四个视界量（Ω_H, κ, A_H, S_Wald）及多个轨道/eikonal量随自旋a改变符号；混合系数呈现与纯GB或纯CS扇区定性不同的自旋依赖行为；结果在小无量纲耦合与中等自旋下保持摄动可控性与数值收敛性。

Conclusion: O(ζ_GB ζ_CS)混合修正不可简化为纯扇区贡献的线性叠加，其诱导的黑洞几何与热力学具有独特自旋结构，显著区别于单扇区EFT预言；该结果为多参数EFT引力检验提供了首个自洽的混合形变基准解，并凸显了在强场观测（如EHT、LISA）中区分宇称混合效应的必要性与可行性。

Abstract: We construct the leading mixed-order $\mathcal{O}(\zeta_{\rm GB}\zeta_{\rm CS})$ deformation of rotating black holes in a combined scalar Gauss-Bonnet and dynamical Chern-Simons effective theory, the sector in which the parity-even and parity-odd corrections act simultaneously. Using a pseudospectral solution of the coupled mixed scalar and metric equations, we compute horizon and equatorial-geodesic observables--the horizon angular velocity, surface gravity, area, and Wald entropy; the innermost stable circular orbit and photon-ring radii; and photon-ring eikonal estimates of the ringdown--at fixed mass and angular momentum over the spin range $0.1\le a\le0.9$, where the solution is numerically convergent and the perturbative expansion is controlled for sufficiently small dimensionless couplings. We verify the exact Kerr and Schwarzschild limits, reproduce available pure-sector benchmarks, and quantify spectral, residual, and extraction uncertainties. The mixed solution satisfies every component of the field equations, including the one that is not imposed in its construction, and its Wald entropy satisfies the first law to about one part in $10^{5}$. The mixed coefficients exhibit spin dependence qualitatively distinct from the individual sectors within this EFT truncation. All four horizon quantities, and several orbital and eikonal ones, change sign as functions of spin. The ringdown results are leading photon-ring eikonal estimates; a full coupled finite-$\ell$ perturbation calculation is left to future work.

</details>


### [112] [Scale-Dependent Weak-Field Dynamics in Horndeski Theory: Galaxy Rotation Curves and Turnaround Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05089)
*Hatice Özer*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Horndeski引力理论中一类与引力波兼容的弱场子类，在星系与星系际尺度上的动力学行为，重点关注非线性标量导数相互作用导致的尺度依赖性引力修正，并分析了其对轨道稳定性、旋转曲线、引力滑移及宇宙学 turnaround 尺度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Horndeski引力中与引力波传播速度一致（c_T = c）的子类在弱场、准静态、近球对称条件下的可观测天体物理效应，以检验其作为暗物质/暗能量替代理论的可行性。

Method: 在弱场近似下，对静止或准球对称背景求解Horndeski理论的运动方程；保留关键的非线性标量梯度项（如G_3(φ,X)∇²φ型），构造有效势、计算圆形轨道稳定性、推导旋转曲线v_rot(r)，比较动力学质量M_dyn与引力透镜质量M_lens，并在大尺度上分析弗里德曼–勒梅特–罗伯逊–沃尔克（FLRW）背景曲率与有效引力的平衡以确定turnaround半径。

Result: 1) 星系尺度：标量场诱导非零引力滑移Ψ ≠ Φ（即Φ和Ψ为两个度规扰动），改变v_rot(r)径向轮廓并影响圆轨道稳定性；2) 星系际尺度：存在由空间曲率与有效引力竞争决定的turnaround半径r_TA ∝ (H₀⁻² Ω_k)^(1/2)，且当r ≫ r_TA时，度规扰动渐近由背景曲率主导，Ψ, Φ → −½ K r²（K为空间曲率）。

Conclusion: 该Horndeski子类虽满足引力波约束，但仍可在星系动力学中产生可观测偏离广义相对论的信号（如引力滑移、异常旋转曲线），并在宇宙学尺度引入曲率驱动的turnaround行为，因此可被星系旋转曲线联合弱引力透镜与大尺度结构观测协同限制。

Abstract: In this work, the weak-field dynamics of a gravitational-wave compatible subclass of Horndeski gravity is investigated on galactic and intergalactic scales. The analysis is performed within the weak-field approximation for stationary and approximately spherically symmetric configurations, retaining the nonlinear scalar derivative interactions responsible for scale-dependent modifications of the gravitational field. On galactic scales, the transition regime is investigated through the effective potential, the stability of circular orbits, galaxy rotation curves, and the relation between dynamical and lensing masses. The analysis shows that the scalar field modifies the orbital dynamics and the radial dependence of the rotational velocity, while generating a nonvanishing gravitational slip between the dynamical and lensing mass estimates. On intergalactic scales, the asymptotic weak-field dynamics is investigated and a turnaround scale is derived from the balance between the effective gravitational attraction and the large-scale curvature contribution. At large distances, the asymptotic gravitational field is governed by the background curvature contribution.

</details>


### [113] [Gravitational-wave lensing beyond the diffraction integral: proper-time methods and the bound lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05152)
*Ginevra Braga,Raul Jimenez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了引力波在强场和弱场下透镜效应的波光学处理，指出标准衍射积分方法依赖于多种近似（几何光学、傍轴近似、薄透镜极限），而通过固有时（worldline）路径积分表述可严格导出这些近似；对点质量透镜，利用Duru-Kleinert规范与KS变换得到精确传播子，并恢复标准放大因子；但该一致性源于库仑势的特殊性，而非近似本身有效；对于束缚透镜（如绕行致密天体的GW源），其时域信号需考虑各世界线独立的推迟时间与多普勒因子，传统单一放大因子失效，且几何/傍轴/静态近似在合相附近及远侧显著失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准引力波透镜分析依赖几何光学与衍射积分，但这些方法隐含eikonal、paraxial、thin-lens等多重近似，其适用范围与物理根源不明确；尤其对动态透镜（如轨道运动的源-透镜系统），现有框架无法描述回波、多普勒调制与延迟演化，亟需基于第一性原理的波光学统一表述。

Method: 采用标量场在弯曲时空中的固有时（proper-time）路径积分表述，将波动方程转化为一阶形式，频率类比为1/ℏ；利用Duru-Kleinert规范与Kustaanheimo-Stiefel坐标变换求解点质量背景下的精确传播子；对束缚透镜系统，严格计算含推迟时间与世界线依赖多普勒因子的时域格林函数，并对比几何光学、衍射积分与静态放大因子的偏差。

Result: 1) 几何光学与衍射积分可从固有时路径积分的驻相条件自然导出，无需人为引入eikonal或paraxial假设；2) 点质量透镜下精确传播子在远场极限下复现标准放大因子，但该一致性源于Schwarzschild度规的库仑型势，而非近似有效性；3) 对束缚透镜，单一时延/放大因子模型完全失效：Shapiro相位遗漏全部高阶衍射贡献；几何光学在w=0.3合相附近丢失46–100%透镜效应；傍轴近似在透镜远侧崩溃；静态放大因子在合相附近误差达≈(1−2)wM/R，其余位置则由回波振幅主导。

Conclusion: 引力波透镜必须基于严格的波光学框架，尤其对动态系统；固有时路径积分提供统一、非近似的起点，揭示标准近似（eikonal、paraxial、thin-lens）的适用边界与物理局限；点质量结果的‘成功’具有误导性，真实透镜（如致密双星）需全息世界线响应建模，放弃单一放大因子范式。

Abstract: Gravitational waves (GWs) can have wavelengths comparable to the Schwarzschild radius of their lenses, and detectors record their phase, so lensing requires wave optics. Its standard tool, the diffraction integral, follows from the particle path integral of a scalar wave on a static weak field, under the eikonal (one-way) and paraxial approximations, plus the thin-lens limit. We give a self-contained account of how the proper-time (worldline) representation overcomes these steps. An auxiliary proper time makes the wave equation exactly first order, with frequency as $1/\hbar$, so geometric optics and, for paraxial thin-lens paths, the diffraction integral follow from stationarity of an exact integral. For a point mass, the Duru-Kleinert (DK) gauge and Kustaanheimo-Stiefel map give the exact propagator; for a distant observer the DK time and fibre angle become the radius and azimuth of a lens plane, the result is the point-lens amplification factor with the offset set by the excess path, and geometric optics requires $w\gg1$ and $wy\gg1$. This agreement reflects the Coulomb potential, not the approximations: the exact eikonal condition, $\varepsilon_E\simeq\bar\lambda/d+GM/c^2d+\mathcal O(\sin^2\theta/2)$, fails near the lens and at large angles, and the one-way step is a non-relativistic limit. We then treat the bound lens, a GW source orbiting its lens, which emits a backscattered echo and moves during the lensing delays. Its exact time-domain signal gives each worldline its own retarded time and Doppler factor, so no single amplification factor describes it. In the diffraction regime the Shapiro phase alone misses all lensing beyond it, geometric optics misses 46-100% of it near conjunction at $w=0.3$, the paraxial geometry fails on the far side, and the static amplification factor along the orbit errs by $\approx(1-2)\,wM/R$ near conjunction and by the echo amplitude elsewhere.

</details>


### [114] [Excitation factors and exceptional points of Kerr--Newman quasinormal modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05201)
*Jun-Kun Zhao,Tieguang Zi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用精确分离的引力-电磁方程研究Kerr-Newman黑洞拟正则模的激发因子与例外点（EPs），发现激发因子随自旋和电荷变化呈现复杂行为，并识别出42个EPs，揭示了非厄米谱中模式共alesce的系统性规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Kerr-Newman黑洞在引力波天文学中的响应特性，特别是旋转与电荷如何影响不同拟正则模的相对激发强度及非厄米谱中模式简并结构。

Method: 基于精确分离的gravitoelectromagnetic方程，数值求解Kerr-Newman黑洞的拟正则频率谱，系统扫描参数空间以定位例外点（EPs），并分析各阶模式（基模与高阶谐波）的激发因子幅值随自旋a和电荷Q的变化行为。

Result: 1) 引力主导模的激发因子幅值随自旋增大而单调减小；电磁主导模则呈现单调与非单调并存行为；2) 一阶泛音激发因子幅值可等于甚至超过基模；3) 共识别42个例外点，其中部分构成按泛音序号递进的序列，趋近极值极限；其余EPs具有相同模式标签但位于不同(a,Q)参数点。

Conclusion: Kerr-Newman黑洞的非厄米拟正则谱中，旋转与电荷协同调控模式激发强度与共alesce拓扑结构；EPs的序列性分布表明其谱几何受极端黑洞极限的深刻约束，为强场引力波建模与参数估计提供关键理论依据。

Abstract: We study excitation factors and investigate exceptional points (EPs) of Kerr--Newman quasinormal modes using the exact separated gravitoelectromagnetic equations. At fixed charge, the magnitudes of the excitation factors for the gravitational-led modes studied here decrease with increasing spin, whereas those for electromagnetic-led modes exhibit both monotonic and nonmonotonic behavior. The magnitude of the excitation factor for the first overtone can be comparable to or larger than that for the fundamental mode. We identify 42 EPs at which pairs of modes coalesce in the gravitational-led and electromagnetic-led sectors. Some EPs form sequences involving successive overtone pairs, with higher-overtone pairs coalescing progressively closer to the extremal limit. The additional EPs identified outside these sequences all share the same mode-pair labels as sequence members, but occur at different values of the black hole spin and charge. These findings clarify how rotation and charge shape the relative excitation strengths of different modes and the pattern of mode coalescence in the non-Hermitian spectrum of Kerr--Newman black holes.

</details>


### [115] [Full-Stokes response to a stochastic gravitational-wave background in pulsar timing arrays: Fully analytic overlap reduction functions at finite distance and coherence of close pulsar pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05219)
*Kun Zhou,Jin Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）对广义相对论中各向异性张量引力波背景的全Stokes响应的完全解析重叠还原函数（ORF），精确包含脉冲星距离与完整传播相位；结果表明不同偏振分量具有不同函数结构（如指数积分、对数、初等函数），并系统分析了零点结构、相干条件、误差敏感性及统计衰减行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升脉冲星计时阵列对各向异性引力波背景的探测灵敏度与建模精度，需超越传统Hellings-Downs近似，构建严格包含传播相位、距离依赖及全Stokes偏振响应的解析框架。

Method: 基于广义相对论中张量型随机引力波背景与脉冲星信号响应的四维时空耦合，严格计算全Stokes响应张量（I, Q, U, V）；利用球谐展开分离天空多极（ℓ ≤ 8），对每个固定ℓ解析求解重叠还原函数，识别其函数类（指数积分E₁、ln、sin/cos等）；结合对称性分析零点结构，引入均匀近似连接长/短波极限，并通过旋转不变性约束横向磁响应主导项。

Result: 获得各向异性张量GW背景下全Stokes ORF的完全解析表达式：强度I与圆偏振V响应含E₁与ln，线偏振Q/U仅含初等函数；发现传播效应可抬升Earth-term零点；揭示即使无偏振背景亦可产生虚部交叉谱；确立脉冲星项相干条件2πfd/c ≲ 1；证明共距误差在同向对中自动抵消，而相对深度误差直接破坏相干；量化低多极范数与总范数随多极升高呈不同高斯平均衰减速率。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了首个兼具严格传播相位、全偏振自由度与各向异性建模能力的PTA解析响应理论，为未来NANOGrav、EPTA、PPTA等实验的偏振分辨搜索、系统误差建模及距离标定提供基准工具，并揭示了引力波背景交叉谱复值性这一新可观测量。

Abstract: We derive fully analytic overlap reduction functions for the full-Stokes response of a pulsar timing array to an anisotropic tensor gravitational-wave background in general relativity. The response retains both pulsar distances and their complete propagation phases. At each fixed finite sky multipole, the intensity and circular polarization responses reduce to finite combinations involving exponential integrals and logarithms, while the electric and magnetic linear polarization responses require only elementary functions. We evaluate all components through $\ell=8$. Symmetry separates exact nulls from additional Earth-term nulls that propagation can lift. Even an unpolarized background can produce an imaginary cross-spectrum. For a pair with three-dimensional separation $d$, the pulsar terms can remain coherent when $2\pi fd/c\lesssim1$, even if both pulsars are many wavelengths from Earth. A uniform approximation retaining all four Earth and pulsar terms connects this regime to the short-wavelength limit, and a rotationally invariant relation fixes the leading transverse magnetic response. Common-distance errors cancel from the aligned pair phase, while relative-depth errors directly affect coherence. Under Gaussian differential averaging, fixed low-multipole norms and the squared norm summed over all multipoles decay at different rates as the response shifts to higher multipoles. Published pulsar configurations illustrate the roles of distance accuracy, bandwidth, and timing fits. At fixed pointwise intensity, a polarization covariance bound and a relative-depth noise budget quantify when differencing suppresses the background. Independent sky integration and matched-accuracy benchmarks test the analytic expressions over the parameter ranges studied.

</details>


### [116] [Dirac stars in Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05240)
*Salvatore Capozziello,Allah Ditta,M. Yousaf,G. Mustafa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Euler-Heisenberg非线性电动力学与U(1)规范场耦合下的自引力旋量场星体（Dirac stars），考虑了四次自相互作用和电荷效应；数值求解了球对称静态构型的场方程，发现电荷增强质量、半径与频率，而EH非线性使星体更致密、最大质量降低，并满足全部能量条件且无事件视界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子电动力学（QED）低能有效理论——Euler-Heisenberg模型——如何修正经典Dirac星体结构，特别是电荷与四次非线性对引力束缚态的质量极限、致密性和稳定性的影响。

Method: 建立含U(1)规范耦合、Euler-Heisenberg四阶非线性电动力学及旋量四次自相互作用的广义相对论作用量；推导球对称静态下的耦合场方程；通过无量纲化将其转化为旋量频率的本征值问题；采用数值方法求解，并系统分析质量-频率曲线、能量条件、压强各向异性与转折点（turning point）以判断稳定性。

Result: 1) 电荷增大星体频率、质量与半径，最大质量从0.709升至1.027（单位M_Pl²/μ）；2) EH非线性削弱星内电场，使同电荷数星体更致密、束缚更强，转折点左移，最大质量在耦合g=0.6时由0.924降至0.840（降幅9%）；3) 弱场下质量修正为−1.5×10⁻⁵(E_max/E_c)²，大耦合对应有效非线性模型而非微扰QED；4) 所有解满足Null、Weak、Strong与Dominant能量条件，具正压强各向异性，无黑洞视界。

Conclusion: Euler-Heisenberg非线性虽不破坏能量条件或产生奇点，但显著改变Dirac星体的平衡结构与最大质量阈值，其效应本质是几何致密化而非简单质量修正；该模型为探索费米子暗物质星体在强场非线性电磁修正下的引力稳定性提供了可控框架。

Abstract: We study self-gravitating configurations of a charged classical spinor field coupled to gravity and to Euler-Heisenberg (EH) nonlinear electrodynamics, truncated at quartic order. The spinor is gauged under a U(1) field and may carry a quartic self-interaction. For static and spherically symmetric configurations we derive the field equations, reduce them to a dimensionless eigenvalue problem for the spinor frequency and solve it numerically. In the uncharged limit our solutions coincide with the known Dirac stars of general relativity. The electric charge increases the frequency, the mass and the size of the stars, and the maximal mass grows from $0.709$ to $1.027$ (in units of $M_{\rm Pl}^2/\mu$) when the charge coupling rises from zero to $0.75$. The quartic term changes the relation between the electric field and the enclosed charge, so that the field inside the star is weaker than in the Maxwell case. Our central result concerns stars with the same number of fermions, and therefore the same total charge. For them the quartic term changes the mass by less than $0.2\%$, but it makes the stars more compact and more strongly bound and shifts the turning point of the family to a smaller particle number. This shift is what lowers the maximal mass, by $9\%$ (from $0.924$ to $0.840$) at charge coupling $0.6$ as the dimensionless EH coupling goes from zero to $200$. In the weak-field regime of quantum electrodynamics (QED) the relative mass correction is $-1.5\times10^{-5}(E_{\max}/E_c)^2$, so the larger couplings describe the quartic model as an effective nonlinear electrodynamics rather than a controlled QED correction. All solutions satisfy the null, weak, strong and dominant energy conditions, show a positive pressure anisotropy, and remain free of horizons. Their stability is discussed through the binding energy and the turning point of the mass curve.

</details>


### [117] [Kerr metric perturbations in Lorenz gauge via numerical relativity methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05259)
*Nami Nishimura,Nils L. Vu,Adrià Gispert-Fernández,Cillian Kelly,Lawrence E. Kidder,Benjamin Leather,Thomas Osburn,Jonathan E. Thompson,Samuel D. Upton,Barry Wardell*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数值相对论的新计算方法，用于求解Kerr黑洞时空中的引力自作用（gravitational self-force）一阶度规扰动，实现了在高自旋（|a|/M=0.998）下圆轨道的指数收敛结果，并为二阶自作用计算奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为LISA任务提供高精度引力波波形模板，需在Kerr背景上完成至少二阶质量比微扰计算；但此前仅在Schwarzschild情形下实现二阶，Kerr情形因爱因斯坦方程非线性复杂性而未突破。

Method: 在Lorenz规范下将线性化爱因斯坦方程按方位角m模展开至频域，构造椭圆型偏微分方程组；采用谱间断Galerkin离散、自适应网格加密与预条件迭代求解器进行数值求解。

Result: 首次实现Kerr时空（自旋达Thorne极限|a|/M=0.998）中赤道圆轨道的一阶引力自作用的指数收敛数值计算，并验证其数据可用于二阶非线性源项构造及二阶扰动求解。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将标量自作用的数值框架推广至完整广义相对论，为Kerr背景下二阶自作用计算提供了可扩展、高精度、数值稳定的计算范式。

Abstract: Gravitational self-force calculations are needed to accurately describe the motion of a small body in a spinning (Kerr) black hole spacetime, which is vital to predict gravitational wave signals from asymmetric-mass compact binaries. Accurate calculations for the upcoming LISA mission require a minimum of second perturbative order in the mass ratio, which has thus far only been achieved for nonspinning (Schwarzschild) black holes due to nonlinear complexities of the Einstein equations. In this work, we calculate Kerr metric perturbations with a new computational approach based on numerical relativity methods, extending recent scalar self-force results to full general relativity. We formulate the perturbative Lorenz-gauge Einstein equations in terms of azimuthal $m$-modes in the frequency domain and solve the resulting elliptic partial differential equations using a spectral discontinuous Galerkin discretization, adaptive mesh refinement, and preconditioned iterative solvers. With our new method, we achieve exponentially convergent first-order gravitational self-force calculations for circular equatorial orbits in Kerr spacetime up to spin $|a|/M=0.998$ (the Thorne limit). Our framework is designed to carry over to second order by delivering accurate and well-behaved first-order data for nonlinear source construction, and as a solver for the second-order metric perturbations themselves.

</details>
